import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import {
  AlertCircle,
  ArrowDown,
  ArrowUp,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronDown,
  Eye,
  Loader2,
  Plus,
  Save,
  Sparkles,
  Trash2,
  Zap,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../api'
import { awards as fallbackAwards } from '@/data/awards'
import { mediaItems as fallbackMedia } from '@/data/media'

/* --------------- helpers (identical to ImpactEditor) --------------- */
const formInput =
  'w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/70 backdrop-blur px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 outline-none transition-all focus:border-emerald-400 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-emerald-500/10 focus:bg-white shadow-sm hover:border-emerald-300 hover:shadow-md'

const labelClass = 'mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600'

const Field = ({ label, children }) => (
  <label className="block">
    <span className={labelClass}>{label}</span>
    {children}
  </label>
)

const IconButton = ({ label, onClick, children, disabled, danger = false }) => (
  <button
    type="button"
    aria-label={label}
    disabled={disabled}
    onClick={onClick}
    className={`inline-flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40 ${
      danger
        ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100 hover:scale-110 active:scale-95'
        : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 hover:scale-110 active:scale-95'
    }`}
  >
    {children}
  </button>
)

const Toast = ({ message, error }) => {
  if (!message && !error) return null
  return (
    <div
      className={`fixed bottom-6 right-6 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-medium shadow-lg transition-all duration-300 ${
        error ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700' : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700'
      }`}
    >
      {error ? <AlertCircle size={18} /> : <CheckCircle2 size={18} />}
      <span>{message || error}</span>
    </div>
  )
}

/* --------------- default item factories --------------- */
const emptyAward = () => ({
  id: `award-${Date.now()}`,
  title: 'New award title',
  organization: '',
  year: new Date().getFullYear(),
  description: '',
  image: '',
  category: 'professional',
})

const emptyMedia = () => ({
  id: `media-${Date.now()}`,
  title: 'New media item',
  type: 'interview',
  source: '',
  date: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
  description: '',
  url: '',
  thumbnail: '',
})

const reorderArray = (arr, idx, dir) => {
  const next = [...arr]
  const target = idx + dir
  if (target < 0 || target >= next.length) return arr
  ;[next[idx], next[target]] = [next[target], next[idx]]
  return next
}

const awardCategories = [
  { value: 'professional', label: 'Professional Excellence' },
  { value: 'alumni', label: 'Alumni Recognition' },
  { value: 'industry', label: 'Industry Honours' },
  { value: 'leadership', label: 'Leadership Awards' },
]

const mediaTypes = [
  { value: 'interview', label: 'Interview' },
  { value: 'conference', label: 'Conference' },
  { value: 'podcast', label: 'Podcast' },
  { value: 'press', label: 'Press' },
  { value: 'speech', label: 'Speech' },
  { value: 'video', label: 'Video' },
]

/* --------------- Main Component --------------- */
export default function AchievementEditor() {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  // Awards section
  const [awardEyebrow, setAwardEyebrow] = useState('Awards & Recognition')
  const [awardTitle, setAwardTitle] = useState('Honours that reflect a life of impact')
  const [awardDescription, setAwardDescription] = useState(
    'Recognition from institutions and peers for sustained excellence in healthcare leadership, service, and innovation.',
  )
  const [awards, setAwards] = useState([])

  // Media section
  const [mediaEyebrow, setMediaEyebrow] = useState('Media & Speaking')
  const [mediaTitle, setMediaTitle] = useState('Voices of leadership')
  const [mediaDescription, setMediaDescription] = useState(
    'Interviews, keynotes, podcasts, and press coverage that amplify the vision for healthcare transformation.',
  )
  const [media, setMedia] = useState([])

  // Expand/collapse tracking
  const [expandedAwards, setExpandedAwards] = useState([])
  const [expandedMedia, setExpandedMedia] = useState([])

  const newRef = useRef(null)
  const [focusId, setFocusId] = useState(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (focusId && newRef.current) {
      newRef.current.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' })
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        newRef.current?.querySelector('input, textarea')?.focus()
      }, 350)
      return () => clearTimeout(timer)
    }
    const reset = setTimeout(() => setFocusId(null), 2500)
    return () => clearTimeout(reset)
  }, [focusId])

  /* --------------- Load data from API --------------- */
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true
    const load = async () => {
      setLoading(true)
      setError('')
      try {
        const res = await api.get('/cms/site-content')
        if (!active) return
        const sections = res.data?.sections || {}

        const awardsData = sections.recognition_awards || {}
        if (awardsData.eyebrow) setAwardEyebrow(awardsData.eyebrow)
        if (awardsData.title) setAwardTitle(awardsData.title)
        if (awardsData.description) setAwardDescription(awardsData.description)
        if (awardsData.blocks?.length) {
          setAwards(
            awardsData.blocks.map((b, idx) => ({
              id: b.id || `award-${idx}`,
              title: b.title || '',
              organization: b.organization || '',
              year: b.year || new Date().getFullYear(),
              description: b.description || '',
              image: b.image || '',
              category: b.category || 'professional',
            })),
          )
        } else {
          setAwards(fallbackAwards.map((a) => ({ ...a })))
        }

        const mediaData = sections.media_speaking || {}
        if (mediaData.eyebrow) setMediaEyebrow(mediaData.eyebrow)
        if (mediaData.title) setMediaTitle(mediaData.title)
        if (mediaData.description) setMediaDescription(mediaData.description)
        if (mediaData.blocks?.length) {
          setMedia(
            mediaData.blocks.map((b, idx) => ({
              id: b.id || `media-${idx}`,
              title: b.title || '',
              type: b.type || 'interview',
              source: b.source || '',
              date: b.date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10),
              description: b.description || '',
              url: b.url || '',
              thumbnail: b.thumbnail || '',
            })),
          )
        } else {
          setMedia(fallbackMedia.map((m) => ({ ...m })))
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Could not load achievements, using fallback data', err)
        setAwards(fallbackAwards.map((a) => ({ ...a })))
        setMedia(fallbackMedia.map((m) => ({ ...m })))
      } finally {
        if (active) setLoading(false)
      }
    }
    load()
    return () => {
      active = false
    }
  }, [])

  /* --------------- Save --------------- */
  const handleSave = async () => {
    setSaving(true)
    setMessage('')
    setError('')
    try {
      await api.put('/cms/sections/recognition_awards', {
        section: {
          eyebrow: awardEyebrow,
          title: awardTitle,
          description: awardDescription,
        },
        blocks: awards.map((item) => ({
          ...item,
          year: Number(item.year) || new Date().getFullYear(),
        })),
      })

      await api.put('/cms/sections/media_speaking', {
        section: {
          eyebrow: mediaEyebrow,
          title: mediaTitle,
          description: mediaDescription,
        },
        blocks: media,
      })

      setMessage('✨ Achievements saved successfully!')
      setTimeout(() => setMessage(''), 3000)
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err)
      setError('Save failed. Please try again.')
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center">
        <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-emerald-500" size={32} />
        <p className="ml-3 text-slate-600">Loading achievements...</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <EditorShell saving={saving} onSave={handleSave} message={message} error={error}>
      {/* ── Awards Hero ── */}
      <Card title="Awards Hero" description="Top section of the Awards page.">
        <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
          <Field label="Eyebrow">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={awardEyebrow}
              onChange={(e) => setAwardEyebrow(e.target.value)}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Title">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={awardTitle}
              onChange={(e) => setAwardTitle(e.target.value)}
            />
          </Field>
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <Field label="Description">
              <textarea
                className={formInput}
                rows={4}
                value={awardDescription}
                onChange={(e) => setAwardDescription(e.target.value)}
              />
            </Field>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* ── Awards List ── */}
      <Card
        title="Awards Entries"
        description="Add, reorder, and edit each award card."
        onAdd={() => {
          const newAward = emptyAward()
          setAwards((prev) => [...prev, newAward])
          setExpandedAwards((prev) => [...prev, awards.length])
          setFocusId(newAward.id)
        }}
      >
        {awards.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState message="No awards yet. Add one to get started." />
        ) : (
          awards.map((award, idx) => (
            <ExpandableItem
              key={award.id}
              idx={idx}
              expanded={expandedAwards}
              setExpanded={setExpandedAwards}
              headerLabel={award.category}
              headerValue={award.title}
              isFocused={award.id === focusId}
              refProp={newRef}
              onUp={() => setAwards((prev) => reorderArray(prev, idx, -1))}
              onDown={() => setAwards((prev) => reorderArray(prev, idx, 1))}
              onDelete={() => {
                setAwards((prev) => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx))
                setExpandedAwards((prev) => prev.filter((id) => id !== String(idx)))
              }}
              upDisabled={idx === 0}
              downDisabled={idx === awards.length - 1}
            >
              <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                <Field label="Title">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={award.title}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const updated = [...awards]
                      updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], title: e.target.value }
                      setAwards(updated)
                    }}
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Organization">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={award.organization}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const updated = [...awards]
                      updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], organization: e.target.value }
                      setAwards(updated)
                    }}
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Year">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    type="number"
                    value={award.year}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const updated = [...awards]
                      updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], year: e.target.value }
                      setAwards(updated)
                    }}
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Category">
                  <select
                    className={formInput}
                    value={award.category}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const updated = [...awards]
                      updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], category: e.target.value }
                      setAwards(updated)
                    }}
                  >
                    {awardCategories.map((cat) => (
                      <option key={cat.value} value={cat.value}>
                        {cat.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </Field>
                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                  <Field label="Description">
                    <textarea
                      className={formInput}
                      rows={4}
                      value={award.description}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const updated = [...awards]
                        updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], description: e.target.value }
                        setAwards(updated)
                      }}
                    />
                  </Field>
                </div>
                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                  <Field label="Image URL">
                    <input
                      className={formInput}
                      value={award.image}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const updated = [...awards]
                        updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], image: e.target.value }
                        setAwards(updated)
                      }}
                      placeholder="/image.png"
                    />
                  </Field>
                </div>
              </div>
            </ExpandableItem>
          ))
        )}
      </Card>

      {/* ── Media Hero ── */}
      <Card title="Media Hero" description="Top section of the Media & Speaking page.">
        <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
          <Field label="Eyebrow">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={mediaEyebrow}
              onChange={(e) => setMediaEyebrow(e.target.value)}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Title">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={mediaTitle}
              onChange={(e) => setMediaTitle(e.target.value)}
            />
          </Field>
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <Field label="Description">
              <textarea
                className={formInput}
                rows={4}
                value={mediaDescription}
                onChange={(e) => setMediaDescription(e.target.value)}
              />
            </Field>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* ── Media List ── */}
      <Card
        title="Media Entries"
        description="Add, reorder, and edit each media or speaking item."
        onAdd={() => {
          const newMedia = emptyMedia()
          setMedia((prev) => [...prev, newMedia])
          setExpandedMedia((prev) => [...prev, media.length])
          setFocusId(newMedia.id)
        }}
      >
        {media.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState message="No media items yet. Add one to get started." />
        ) : (
          media.map((item, idx) => (
            <ExpandableItem
              key={item.id}
              idx={idx}
              expanded={expandedMedia}
              setExpanded={setExpandedMedia}
              headerLabel={item.type}
              headerValue={item.title}
              isFocused={item.id === focusId}
              refProp={newRef}
              onUp={() => setMedia((prev) => reorderArray(prev, idx, -1))}
              onDown={() => setMedia((prev) => reorderArray(prev, idx, 1))}
              onDelete={() => {
                setMedia((prev) => prev.filter((_, i) => i !== idx))
                setExpandedMedia((prev) => prev.filter((id) => id !== String(idx)))
              }}
              upDisabled={idx === 0}
              downDisabled={idx === media.length - 1}
            >
              <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                <Field label="Title">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={item.title}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const updated = [...media]
                      updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], title: e.target.value }
                      setMedia(updated)
                    }}
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Type">
                  <select
                    className={formInput}
                    value={item.type}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const updated = [...media]
                      updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], type: e.target.value }
                      setMedia(updated)
                    }}
                  >
                    {mediaTypes.map((type) => (
                      <option key={type.value} value={type.value}>
                        {type.label}
                      </option>
                    ))}
                  </select>
                </Field>
                <Field label="Source">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={item.source}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const updated = [...media]
                      updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], source: e.target.value }
                      setMedia(updated)
                    }}
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Date">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    type="date"
                    value={item.date}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const updated = [...media]
                      updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], date: e.target.value }
                      setMedia(updated)
                    }}
                  />
                </Field>
                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                  <Field label="Description">
                    <textarea
                      className={formInput}
                      rows={4}
                      value={item.description}
                      onChange={(e) => {
                        const updated = [...media]
                        updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], description: e.target.value }
                        setMedia(updated)
                      }}
                    />
                  </Field>
                </div>
                <Field label="Source URL">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={item.url || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const updated = [...media]
                      updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], url: e.target.value }
                      setMedia(updated)
                    }}
                    placeholder="https://..."
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Thumbnail URL">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={item.thumbnail || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => {
                      const updated = [...media]
                      updated[idx] = { ...updated[idx], thumbnail: e.target.value }
                      setMedia(updated)
                    }}
                    placeholder="/thumbnail.jpg"
                  />
                </Field>
              </div>
            </ExpandableItem>
          ))
        )}
      </Card>
    </EditorShell>
  )
}

/* --------------- Reusable sub‑components --------------- */
function EditorShell({ saving, onSave, message, error, children }) {
  return (
    <section className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 to-slate-100/60">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-10">
        <div className="mb-10 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-6">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-600 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Zap size={14} /> Achievements Editor
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">Awards & Media</h1>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onSave}
            disabled={saving}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-emerald-600 px-6 py-3 font-semibold text-white transition-all hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-emerald-500/30 active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {saving ? <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" /> : <Save size={18} />}
            <span>{saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save All Changes'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_320px]">
          <div className="space-y-8">{children}</div>

          <aside className="space-y-6">
            <div className="sticky top-8 space-y-6">
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-md p-6 shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg transition-all">
                <h3 className="mb-4 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Eye size={14} className="text-emerald-500" /> Live Preview
                </h3>
                <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-6 text-slate-900 min-h-[160px]">
                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500">
                    AWARDS & MEDIA
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
                    All changes are stored in the database.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50 to-emerald-100 p-6">
                <h3 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-emerald-900">
                  <Sparkles size={16} className="text-emerald-600" /> Editing Guide
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-emerald-800">
                  Click <strong>Save All Changes</strong> to persist your edits.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
      <Toast message={message} error={error} />
    </section>
  )
}

function Card({ title, description, onAdd, children }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:shadow-lg hover:border-emerald-300">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-6">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{title}</h2>
          {description && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">{description}</p>}
        </div>
        {onAdd && (
          <button
            onClick={onAdd}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-emerald-600 px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-white transition-all hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-emerald-500/30 active:scale-95"
          >
            <Plus size={18} /> Add
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}

function ExpandableItem({
  idx,
  expanded,
  setExpanded,
  headerLabel,
  headerValue,
  children,
  onUp,
  onDown,
  onDelete,
  upDisabled,
  downDisabled,
  isFocused,
  refProp,
}) {
  const isOpen = expanded.includes(String(idx))
  return (
    <div
      ref={isFocused ? refProp : null}
      className={`rounded-2xl border bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm shadow-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:shadow-lg hover:border-emerald-300 hover:scale-[1.01] cursor-pointer ${
        isFocused
          ? 'border-emerald-400 ring-2 ring-emerald-400/30 shadow-xl shadow-emerald-100/50'
          : 'border-slate-200'
      }`}
    >
      <button
        onClick={() =>
          setExpanded((prev) =>
            prev.includes(String(idx))
              ? prev.filter((i) => i !== String(idx))
              : [...prev, String(idx)],
          )
        }
        className="flex w-full items-center justify-between p-5 text-left"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-400 to-emerald-600 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-md">
            {String(idx + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
          </span>
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-600">
              {headerLabel}
            </p>
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 line-clamp-1">{headerValue}</h3>
          </div>
        </div>
        <ChevronDown
          size={18}
          className={`text-slate-400 transition-transform duration-300 ${isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`}
        />
      </button>

      <div
        className={`overflow-hidden transition-all duration-500 ease-in-out ${
          isOpen ? 'max-h-[900px] opacity-100' : 'max-h-0 opacity-0'
        }`}
      >
        <div className="border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50 p-5 space-y-4">
          {children}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <IconButton label="Move up" disabled={upDisabled} onClick={onUp}>
                <ArrowUp size={16} />
              </IconButton>
              <IconButton label="Move down" disabled={downDisabled} onClick={onDown}>
                <ArrowDown size={16} />
              </IconButton>
            </div>
            <IconButton label="Delete" danger onClick={onDelete}>
              <Trash2 size={16} />
            </IconButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function EmptyState({ message }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-6 py-12 text-center hover:border-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-50 transition-all">
      <p className="text-slate-600">{message}</p>
    </div>
  )
}



import { ArrowDown, ArrowUp, Loader2, Plus, Save, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../api'

const emptyIdentity = { label: '', title: '', description: '', icon: '', image_url: '', sort_order: 0, is_active: 1 }
const emptyTimeline = {
  slug: '',
  year: '',
  title: '',
  subtitle: '',
  description: '',
  category: 'milestone',
  icon: '',
  sort_order: 0,
  is_active: 1,
}
const emptyNav = { parent_id: '', label: '', path: '', target: '_self', sort_order: 0, is_active: 1 }

const flattenNavigation = (items, depth = 0) =>
  items.flatMap((item) => [
    { ...item, depth },
    ...flattenNavigation(item.children || [], depth + 1),
  ])

const formInput =
  'w-full border border-rule bg-paper px-3 py-2.5 text-sm text-ink outline-none transition-colors placeholder:text-ink-faint focus:border-teal'
const labelClass = 'mb-1.5 block font-mono text-[9px] uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-ink-muted'

function Field({ label, children }) {
  return (
    <label>
      <span className={labelClass}>{label}</span>
      {children}
    </label>
  )
}

function IconButton({ label, onClick, children, disabled, danger = false }) {
  return (
    <button
      type="button"
      aria-label={label}
      title={label}
      disabled={disabled}
      onClick={onClick}
      className={`inline-flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center border transition-colors disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40 ${
        danger
          ? 'border-red-200 text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50'
          : 'border-rule text-ink-muted hover:bg-paper-warm hover:text-ink'
      }`}
    >
      {children}
    </button>
  )
}

export default function CmsEditor() {
  const [content, setContent] = useState(null)
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true)
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const navigationFlat = useMemo(() => flattenNavigation(content?.navigation || []), [content])

  const loadContent = async () => {
    setIsLoading(true)
    setError('')
    try {
      const response = await api.get('/cms/site-content')
      setContent(response.data)
    } catch {
      setError('Could not load CMS content.')
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false)
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadContent()
  }, [])

  const updateSection = (slug, patch) => {
    setContent((current) => ({
      ...current,
      sections: {
        ...current.sections,
        [slug]: { ...current.sections[slug], ...patch },
      },
    }))
  }

  const updateSectionBlock = (slug, index, patch) => {
    setContent((current) => {
      const section = current.sections[slug]
      const blocks = section.blocks.map((block, blockIndex) =>
        blockIndex === index ? { ...block, ...patch } : block,
      )
      return {
        ...current,
        sections: { ...current.sections, [slug]: { ...section, blocks } },
      }
    })
  }

  const saveSection = async (slug) => {
    setMessage('')
    setError('')
    setIsSaving(true)
    try {
      const section = content.sections[slug]
      await api.put(`/cms/sections/${slug}`, { section, blocks: section.blocks })
      setMessage('Section saved.')
      await loadContent()
    } catch {
      setError('Could not save section.')
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false)
    }
  }

  const updateCollectionItem = (collection, id, patch) => {
    setContent((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [collection]: current[collection].map((item) => (item.id === id ? { ...item, ...patch } : item)),
    }))
  }

  const reorderCollection = (collection, id, direction) => {
    setContent((current) => {
      const list = [...current[collection]]
      const index = list.findIndex((item) => item.id === id)
      const target = index + direction
      if (target < 0 || target >= list.length) return current
      const moved = list[index]
      list[index] = list[target]
      list[target] = moved
      return {
        ...current,
        [collection]: list.map((item, sortIndex) => ({ ...item, sort_order: sortIndex + 1 })),
      }
    })
  }

  const saveCollection = async (collection, endpoint) => {
    setMessage('')
    setError('')
    setIsSaving(true)
    try {
      for (const item of content[collection]) {
        if (typeof item.id === 'number') {
          await api.put(`/cms/${endpoint}/${item.id}`, item)
        } else {
          const { id, ...payload } = item
          await api.post(`/cms/${endpoint}`, payload)
        }
      }
      setMessage('Collection saved.')
      await loadContent()
    } catch {
      setError('Could not save collection.')
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false)
    }
  }

  const deleteItem = async (endpoint, id) => {
    setMessage('')
    setError('')
    try {
      await api.delete(`/cms/${endpoint}/${id}`)
      setMessage('Item deleted.')
      await loadContent()
    } catch {
      setError('Could not delete item.')
    }
  }

  const addCollectionItem = (collection, emptyItem) => {
    setContent((current) => ({
      ...current,
      [collection]: [...current[collection], { ...emptyItem, id: `new-${Date.now()}`, sort_order: current[collection].length + 1 }],
    }))
  }

  const updateNavigationItem = (id, patch) => {
    const updateTree = (items) =>
      items.map((item) => {
        if (item.id === id) return { ...item, ...patch }
        return { ...item, children: updateTree(item.children || []) }
      })

    setContent((current) => ({ ...current, navigation: updateTree(current.navigation) }))
  }

  const addNavigationItem = () => {
    setContent((current) => ({
      ...current,
      navigation: [
        ...current.navigation,
        { ...emptyNav, id: `new-${Date.now()}`, children: [], sort_order: current.navigation.length + 1 },
      ],
    }))
  }

  const saveNavigation = async () => {
    setMessage('')
    setError('')
    setIsSaving(true)
    try {
      for (const item of flattenNavigation(content.navigation)) {
        const payload = {
          parent_id: item.parent_id || null,
          label: item.label,
          path: item.path || null,
          target: item.target || '_self',
          sort_order: item.sort_order || 0,
          is_active: item.is_active === 0 ? 0 : 1,
        }
        if (typeof item.id === 'number') {
          await api.put(`/cms/navigation-items/${item.id}`, payload)
        } else {
          await api.post('/cms/navigation-items', payload)
        }
      }
      setMessage('Navigation saved.')
      await loadContent()
    } catch {
      setError('Could not save navigation.')
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false)
    }
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-80 items-center justify-center border border-rule bg-paper-card">
        <Loader2 size={26} className="animate-spin text-teal" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  const vision = content.sections.vision_agenda
  const aboutVision = content.sections.about_vision
  const aboutBanner = content.sections.about_banner
  const aboutStory = content.sections.about_story

  const addSectionBlock = (slug, emptyBlock) => {
    setContent((current) => ({
      ...current,
      sections: {
        ...current.sections,
        [slug]: {
          ...current.sections[slug],
          blocks: [...(current.sections[slug].blocks || []), { ...emptyBlock, id: `new-${Date.now()}`, sort_order: (current.sections[slug].blocks || []).length + 1 }],
        },
      },
    }))
  }

  const removeSectionBlock = (slug, index) => {
    setContent((current) => ({
      ...current,
      sections: {
        ...current.sections,
        [slug]: { ...current.sections[slug], blocks: current.sections[slug].blocks.filter((_, i) => i !== index) },
      },
    }))
  }

  const reorderSectionBlocks = (slug, index, direction) => {
    setContent((current) => {
      const next = [...(current.sections[slug].blocks || [])]
      const target = index + direction
      if (target < 0 || target >= next.length) return current
      const item = next[index]
      next[index] = next[target]
      next[target] = item
      return {
        ...current,
        sections: { ...current.sections, [slug]: { ...current.sections[slug], blocks: next.map((b, i) => ({ ...b, sort_order: i + 1 })) } },
      }
    })
  }

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto max-w-7xl space-y-8">
      <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 border-b border-rule pb-7 lg:flex-row lg:items-end">
        <div>
          <p className="section-tag">CMS Editor</p>
          <h1 className="mt-3 font-serif text-4xl font-light text-ink sm:text-5xl">Live Website Content</h1>
        </div>
        <div className="text-sm font-light text-ink-mid">
          {message && <p className="text-teal">{message}</p>}
          {error && <p className="text-red-700">{error}</p>}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="border border-rule bg-paper-card p-5 sm:p-6">
        <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-rule pb-4">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl font-light text-ink">Vision Agenda</h2>
          <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-teal px-4 py-2.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-white" onClick={() => saveSection('vision_agenda')} disabled={isSaving}>
            <Save size={15} /> Save
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
          <Field label="Eyebrow"><input className={formInput} value={vision.eyebrow || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSection('vision_agenda', { eyebrow: e.target.value })} /></Field>
          <Field label="Profile Label"><input className={formInput} value={vision.subtitle || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSection('vision_agenda', { subtitle: e.target.value })} /></Field>
          <Field label="Image URL"><input className={formInput} value={vision.image_url || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSection('vision_agenda', { image_url: e.target.value })} /></Field>
          <Field label="Image Alt"><input className={formInput} value={vision.image_alt || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSection('vision_agenda', { image_alt: e.target.value })} /></Field>
          <Field label="Title HTML"><textarea className={`${formInput} md:col-span-2`} rows={4} value={vision.title || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSection('vision_agenda', { title: e.target.value })} /></Field>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-5 grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-3">
          {vision.blocks.map((block, index) => (
            <div key={block.id || index} className="border border-rule bg-paper p-4">
              <Field label="Label"><input className={formInput} value={block.eyebrow || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSectionBlock('vision_agenda', index, { eyebrow: e.target.value })} /></Field>
              <div className="mt-3">
                <Field label="Title"><input className={formInput} value={block.title || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSectionBlock('vision_agenda', index, { title: e.target.value })} /></Field>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {aboutBanner && (
        <div className="border border-rule bg-paper-card p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-rule pb-4">
            <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl font-light text-ink">About Page Banner</h2>
            <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-teal px-4 py-2.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-white" onClick={() => saveSection('about_banner')} disabled={isSaving}>
              <Save size={15} /> Save
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
            <Field label="Title"><input className={formInput} value={aboutBanner.title || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSection('about_banner', { title: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            <div className="md:col-span-2">
              <Field label="Description"><textarea className={formInput} rows={3} value={aboutBanner.description || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSection('about_banner', { description: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {aboutStory && (
        <div className="border border-rule bg-paper-card p-5 sm:p-6">
          <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-rule pb-4">
            <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl font-light text-ink">About Story</h2>
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-mid">The About Story editor has moved to the Profile → Journey editor to avoid duplication.</p>
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-mid mt-2">Edit it here: <code>/admin/profile/journey</code></p>
        </div>
      )}

      <CollectionEditor
        title="Identity Items"
        items={content.identityItems}
        onAdd={() => addCollectionItem('identityItems', emptyIdentity)}
        onSave={() => saveCollection('identityItems', 'identity-items')}
        onDelete={(id) => deleteItem('identity-items', id)}
        onMove={(id, direction) => reorderCollection('identityItems', id, direction)}
        renderItem={(item) => (
          <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-[80px_minmax(0,1fr)]">
            <Field label="Number"><input className={formInput} value={item.label || ''} onChange={(e) => updateCollectionItem('identityItems', item.id, { label: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            <Field label="Title"><input className={formInput} value={item.title || ''} onChange={(e) => updateCollectionItem('identityItems', item.id, { title: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            <div className="md:col-span-2">
              <Field label="Description"><textarea className={formInput} rows={3} value={item.description || ''} onChange={(e) => updateCollectionItem('identityItems', item.id, { description: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      />

      <CollectionEditor
        title="Timeline Events"
        items={content.timelineEvents}
        onAdd={() => addCollectionItem('timelineEvents', emptyTimeline)}
        onSave={() => saveCollection('timelineEvents', 'timeline-events')}
        onDelete={(id) => deleteItem('timeline-events', id)}
        onMove={(id, direction) => reorderCollection('timelineEvents', id, direction)}
        renderItem={(item) => (
          <div className="grid gap-3 md:grid-cols-[90px_minmax(0,1fr)_160px]">
            <Field label="Year"><input className={formInput} value={item.year || ''} onChange={(e) => updateCollectionItem('timelineEvents', item.id, { year: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            <Field label="Title"><input className={formInput} value={item.title || ''} onChange={(e) => updateCollectionItem('timelineEvents', item.id, { title: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            <Field label="Category"><input className={formInput} value={item.category || ''} onChange={(e) => updateCollectionItem('timelineEvents', item.id, { category: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            <Field label="Subtitle"><input className={formInput} value={item.subtitle || ''} onChange={(e) => updateCollectionItem('timelineEvents', item.id, { subtitle: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            <div className="md:col-span-2">
              <Field label="Description"><textarea className={formInput} rows={3} value={item.description || ''} onChange={(e) => updateCollectionItem('timelineEvents', item.id, { description: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      />

      <div className="border border-rule bg-paper-card p-5 sm:p-6">
        <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-rule pb-4">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl font-light text-ink">About Vision Quote</h2>
          <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-teal px-4 py-2.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-white" onClick={() => saveSection('about_vision')} disabled={isSaving}>
            <Save size={15} /> Save
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
          <Field label="Eyebrow"><input className={formInput} value={aboutVision.eyebrow || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSection('about_vision', { eyebrow: e.target.value })} /></Field>
          <Field label="Title"><input className={formInput} value={aboutVision.title || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSection('about_vision', { title: e.target.value })} /></Field>
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <Field label="Quote"><textarea className={formInput} rows={4} value={aboutVision.description || ''} onChange={(e) => updateSection('about_vision', { description: e.target.value })} /></Field>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="border border-rule bg-paper-card p-5 sm:p-6">
        <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-rule pb-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
          <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl font-light text-ink">Navigation</h2>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 border border-rule px-4 py-2.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-ink" onClick={addNavigationItem}>
              <Plus size={15} /> Add
            </button>
            <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-teal px-4 py-2.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-white" onClick={saveNavigation} disabled={isSaving}>
              <Save size={15} /> Save
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-3">
          {navigationFlat.map((item) => (
            <div key={item.id} className="grid gap-3 border border-rule bg-paper p-4 md:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_minmax(0,1fr)_120px_120px]">
              <div style={{ paddingLeft: item.depth * 20 }}>
                <Field label="Label"><input className={formInput} value={item.label || ''} onChange={(e) => updateNavigationItem(item.id, { label: e.target.value })} /></Field>
              </div>
              <Field label="Path"><input className={formInput} value={item.path || ''} onChange={(e) => updateNavigationItem(item.id, { path: e.target.value })} /></Field>
              <Field label="Parent">
                <select className={formInput} value={item.parent_id || ''} onChange={(e) => updateNavigationItem(item.id, { parent_id: e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : null })}>
                  <option value="">Top level</option>
                  {navigationFlat.filter((candidate) => candidate.id !== item.id).map((candidate) => (
                    <option key={candidate.id} value={candidate.id}>{`${'--'.repeat(candidate.depth)} ${candidate.label}`}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
              </Field>
              <Field label="Order"><input className={formInput} type="number" value={item.sort_order || 0} onChange={(e) => updateNavigationItem(item.id, { sort_order: Number(e.target.value) })} /></Field>
              {typeof item.id === 'number' && (
                <div className="md:col-span-4">
                  <IconButton label="Delete" danger onClick={() => deleteItem('navigation-items', item.id)}><Trash2 size={15} /></IconButton>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

function CollectionEditor({ title, items, onAdd, onSave, onDelete, onMove, renderItem }) {
  return (
    <div className="border border-rule bg-paper-card p-5 sm:p-6">
      <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-3 border-b border-rule pb-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center sm:justify-between">
        <h2 className="font-serif text-3xl font-light text-ink">{title}</h2>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 border border-rule px-4 py-2.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-ink" onClick={onAdd}>
            <Plus size={15} /> Add
          </button>
          <button className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 bg-teal px-4 py-2.5 text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.16em] text-white" onClick={onSave}>
            <Save size={15} /> Save
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="space-y-3">
        {items.map((item, index) => (
          <div key={item.id} className="grid gap-4 border border-rule bg-paper p-4 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_120px]">
            {renderItem(item)}
            <div className="flex items-start gap-2 lg:justify-end">
              <IconButton label="Move up" disabled={index === 0} onClick={() => onMove(item.id, -1)}><ArrowUp size={15} /></IconButton>
              <IconButton label="Move down" disabled={index === items.length - 1} onClick={() => onMove(item.id, 1)}><ArrowDown size={15} /></IconButton>
              {typeof item.id === 'number' && <IconButton label="Delete" danger onClick={() => onDelete(item.id)}><Trash2 size={15} /></IconButton>}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import {
  Loader2,
  Save,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  ArrowUp,
  ArrowDown,
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronDown,
  Zap,
  Upload,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../api'

// ------------------------- helpers -------------------------
const formInput =
  'w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/70 backdrop-blur px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 outline-none transition-all focus:border-emerald-400 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-emerald-500/10 focus:bg-white shadow-sm hover:border-emerald-300 hover:shadow-md'

const labelClass = 'mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600'

const Field = ({ label, children, hint }) => (
  <label className="block">
    <span className={labelClass}>{label}</span>
    {children}
    {hint && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">{hint}</p>}
  </label>
)

const IconButton = ({ label, onClick, children, disabled, danger = false }) => (
  <button
    type="button"
    aria-label={label}
    title={label}
    disabled={disabled}
    onClick={onClick}
    className={`inline-flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40 ${
      danger
        ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100 hover:scale-110 active:scale-95'
        : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200 hover:scale-110 active:scale-95'
    }`}
  >
    {children}
  </button>
)

const Toast = ({ message, error }) => {
  if (!message && !error) return null
  return (
    <div
      className={`fixed bottom-6 right-6 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-medium shadow-lg transition-all duration-300 ${
        error ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700' : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700'
      }`}
    >
      {error ? <AlertCircle size={18} /> : <CheckCircle2 size={18} />}
      <span>{message || error}</span>
    </div>
  )
}

const readLocalImage = (file, onLoad, onError) => {
  if (!file) return
  const reader = new FileReader()
  reader.onload = () => onLoad(String(reader.result || ''))
  reader.onerror = onError
  reader.readAsDataURL(file)
}

// ------------------------- defaults -------------------------
const defaultSettings = {
  heading: 'Get in touch',
  email: 'info@liyanahealthcare.com',
  phone: '+251-11-XXX-XXXX',
  location: 'Addis Ababa, Ethiopia',
  image_url: '',
  image_caption: '',
}

const defaultInquiryTypes = [
  { value: 'partnership', label: 'Partnerships' },
  { value: 'media', label: 'Media' },
  { value: 'speaking', label: 'Speaking' },
  { value: 'other', label: 'Policy' },
  { value: 'foundation', label: 'Foundation' },
]

// ------------------------- main -------------------------
export default function ContactEditor() {
  const [settings, setSettings] = useState(defaultSettings)
  const [inquiryTypes, setInquiryTypes] = useState(defaultInquiryTypes)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [deletedTypeIds, setDeletedTypeIds] = useState([])
  const [expandedTypes, setExpandedTypes] = useState([])
  const [newItemId, setNewItemId] = useState(null)
  const newItemRef = useRef(null)

  const toggleType = (id) =>
    setExpandedTypes((prev) => (prev.includes(id) ? prev.filter((i) => i !== id) : [...prev, id]))

  useEffect(() => {
    ;(async () => {
      try {
        const [settingsRes, typesRes] = await Promise.all([
          api.get('/contact/settings'),
          api.get('/contact/inquiry-types'),
        ])
        if (settingsRes.data && Object.keys(settingsRes.data).length)
          setSettings({ ...defaultSettings, ...settingsRes.data })
        if (typesRes.data?.length)
          setInquiryTypes(typesRes.data.map((t) => ({ id: t.id, value: t.value, label: t.label })))
      } catch {
        setError('Failed to load contact data – using defaults.')
      } finally {
        setLoading(false)
      }
    })()
  }, [])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (newItemId && newItemRef.current) {
      newItemRef.current.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' })
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        const firstInput = newItemRef.current?.querySelector('input')
        firstInput?.focus()
      }, 350)
      const reset = setTimeout(() => setNewItemId(null), 2500)
      return () => {
        clearTimeout(timer)
        clearTimeout(reset)
      }
    }
  }, [newItemId])

  const updateField = (field, value) => setSettings((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: value }))
  const handleImageUpload = (file) =>
    readLocalImage(
      file,
      (url) => updateField('image_url', url),
      () => setError('Could not read image.'),
    )

  const addInquiryType = () => {
    const tempId = `temp-${Date.now()}`
    setInquiryTypes((prev) => [...prev, { id: tempId, label: 'New Type', value: 'new_type' }])
    setExpandedTypes((prev) => [...prev, tempId])
    setNewItemId(tempId)
  }

  const updateInquiryTypeLocal = (id, patch) =>
    setInquiryTypes((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === id ? { ...t, ...patch } : t)))
  const deleteTypeLocal = (id) => {
    if (typeof id === 'number') setDeletedTypeIds((prev) => [...prev, id])
    setInquiryTypes((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== id))
  }
  const reorderTypes = (index, dir) =>
    setInquiryTypes((prev) => {
      const next = [...prev]
      const target = index + dir
      if (target < 0 || target >= next.length) return prev
      ;[next[index], next[target]] = [next[target], next[index]]
      return next
    })

  const saveAll = async () => {
    setSaving(true)
    setMessage('')
    setError('')
    try {
      await api.put('/contact/settings', settings)
      for (const id of deletedTypeIds) await api.delete(`/contact/inquiry-types/${id}`)
      for (const type of inquiryTypes) {
        const payload = { label: type.label, value: type.value }
        if (typeof type.id === 'number') await api.put(`/contact/inquiry-types/${type.id}`, payload)
        else await api.post('/contact/inquiry-types', payload)
      }
      const [settingsRes, typesRes] = await Promise.all([
        api.get('/contact/settings'),
        api.get('/contact/inquiry-types'),
      ])
      setSettings({ ...defaultSettings, ...settingsRes.data })
      setInquiryTypes(
        (typesRes.data || []).map((t) => ({ id: t.id, value: t.value, label: t.label })),
      )
      setDeletedTypeIds([])
      setMessage('✨ All contact data saved beautifully!')
      setTimeout(() => setMessage(''), 3000)
    } catch {
      setError('Could not save. Please try again.')
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 to-slate-100/60">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
          <div className="relative w-16 h-16">
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-400 to-emerald-600 rounded-full animate-spin opacity-20" />
            <Loader2 size={32} className="animate-spin text-emerald-500 absolute inset-0 m-auto" />
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 animate-pulse">Loading contact settings...</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <section className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 to-slate-100/60">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-10">
        <div className="mb-10 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-6">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-600 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Zap size={14} /> Contact Editor
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900 sm:text-5xl">
              Contact Page Settings
            </h1>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={saveAll}
            disabled={saving}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-emerald-600 px-6 py-3 font-semibold text-white transition-all hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-emerald-500/30 active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed hover:scale-105 duration-200"
          >
            {saving ? <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" /> : <Save size={18} />}
            <span>{saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save All Changes'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_320px]">
          <div className="space-y-8">
            {/* Contact Details Card */}
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:shadow-lg hover:border-emerald-300">
              <div className="mb-6 border-b border-slate-200 pb-6">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">Contact Details</h2>
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">Update the main contact information.</p>
              </div>
              <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
                <Field label="Heading">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={settings.heading}
                    onChange={(e) => updateField('heading', e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Get in Touch"
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Email">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={settings.email}
                    onChange={(e) => updateField('email', e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="hello@example.com"
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Phone">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={settings.phone}
                    onChange={(e) => updateField('phone', e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="+123 456 7890"
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Location">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={settings.location}
                    onChange={(e) => updateField('location', e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="City, Country"
                  />
                </Field>
                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                  <Field label="Image URL">
                    <div className="flex gap-2">
                      <input
                        className={formInput}
                        value={settings.image_url}
                        onChange={(e) => updateField('image_url', e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="https://..."
                      />
                      <label className="inline-flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 transition-all hover:bg-slate-200 hover:scale-110">
                        <Upload size={18} />
                        <input
                          type="file"
                          accept="image/*"
                          className="sr-only"
                          onChange={(e) => handleImageUpload(e.target.files?.[0])}
                        />
                      </label>
                    </div>
                  </Field>
                </div>
                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                  <Field label="Image Caption">
                    <input
                      className={formInput}
                      value={settings.image_caption}
                      onChange={(e) => updateField('image_caption', e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Our office in Addis Ababa"
                    />
                  </Field>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Inquiry Types Card */}
            <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:shadow-lg hover:border-emerald-300">
              <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-6">
                <div>
                  <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">Inquiry Types</h2>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
                    Manage inquiry options shown on the contact form.
                  </p>
                </div>
                <button
                  onClick={addInquiryType}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-emerald-600 px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-white transition-all hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-emerald-500/30 active:scale-95"
                >
                  <Plus size={18} /> Add Type
                </button>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-3">
                {inquiryTypes.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-6 py-12 text-center hover:border-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-50 transition-all">
                    <p className="text-slate-600">No inquiry types yet. Add one to get started.</p>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  inquiryTypes.map((type, idx) => {
                    const isOpen = expandedTypes.includes(type.id)
                    const isNew = type.id === newItemId
                    return (
                      <div
                        key={type.id}
                        ref={isNew ? newItemRef : null}
                        className={`rounded-2xl border bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm shadow-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:shadow-lg hover:border-emerald-300 hover:scale-[1.01] cursor-pointer ${
                          isNew
                            ? 'border-emerald-400 ring-2 ring-emerald-400/30 shadow-xl shadow-emerald-100/50'
                            : 'border-slate-200'
                        }`}
                      >
                        <button
                          onClick={() => toggleType(type.id)}
                          className="flex w-full items-center justify-between p-5 text-left"
                        >
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                            <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-400 to-emerald-600 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-md">
                              {String(idx + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
                            </span>
                            <div>
                              <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-600">
                                {type.value}
                              </p>
                              <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 line-clamp-1">
                                {type.label}
                              </h3>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                          <ChevronDown
                            size={18}
                            className={`text-slate-400 transition-transform duration-300 ${isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`}
                          />
                        </button>

                        <div
                          className={`overflow-hidden transition-all duration-500 ease-in-out ${isOpen ? 'max-h-[500px] opacity-100' : 'max-h-0 opacity-0'}`}
                        >
                          <div className="border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50 p-5 space-y-4">
                            <Field label="Label">
                              <input
                                className={formInput}
                                value={type.label}
                                onChange={(e) =>
                                  updateInquiryTypeLocal(type.id, {
                                    label: e.target.value,
                                    value: e.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '_'),
                                  })
                                }
                              />
                            </Field>
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
                              <div className="flex gap-2">
                                <IconButton
                                  label="Move up"
                                  disabled={idx === 0}
                                  onClick={() => reorderTypes(idx, -1)}
                                >
                                  <ArrowUp size={16} />
                                </IconButton>
                                <IconButton
                                  label="Move down"
                                  disabled={idx === inquiryTypes.length - 1}
                                  onClick={() => reorderTypes(idx, 1)}
                                >
                                  <ArrowDown size={16} />
                                </IconButton>
                              </div>
                              <IconButton
                                label="Delete"
                                danger
                                onClick={() => deleteTypeLocal(type.id)}
                              >
                                <Trash2 size={16} />
                              </IconButton>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    )
                  })
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* right preview */}
          <aside className="space-y-6">
            <div className="sticky top-8 space-y-6">
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-md p-6 shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg transition-all">
                <h3 className="mb-4 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Zap size={14} className="text-emerald-500" /> Live Preview
                </h3>
                <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-6 hover:bg-emerald-50 hover:border-emerald-300 transition-all">
                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-400">
                    Contact Page
                  </p>
                  <h4 className="mt-3 text-lg font-bold leading-snug text-slate-900">
                    {settings.heading || 'Get in Touch'}
                  </h4>
                  <div className="mt-4 space-y-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
                    {settings.email && <p>📧 {settings.email}</p>}
                    {settings.phone && <p>📞 {settings.phone}</p>}
                    {settings.location && <p>📍 {settings.location}</p>}
                  </div>
                  {settings.image_url && (
                    <div className="mt-4 overflow-hidden rounded-lg border border-slate-200 hover:shadow-md transition-all">
                      <img src={settings.image_url} alt="" className="h-32 w-full object-cover" />
                      {settings.image_caption && (
                        <p className="p-2 text-xs text-slate-500">{settings.image_caption}</p>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  )}
                  <div className="mt-4 border-t border-slate-200 pt-3">
                    <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400">Inquiry Types:</p>
                    <ul className="mt-2 space-y-1">
                      {inquiryTypes.map((t) => (
                        <li key={t.id} className="text-sm text-slate-700">
                          <span className="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-emerald-500 inline-block mr-2" />
                          {t.label}
                        </li>
                      ))}
                    </ul>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50 to-emerald-100 p-6 hover:shadow-lg hover:border-emerald-300 transition-all">
                <h3 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-emerald-900">
                  <Sparkles size={16} className="text-emerald-600" /> Editing Guide
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-emerald-800">
                  Changes are local until you click <strong>Save All Changes</strong>. This saves
                  settings and inquiry types to the CMS.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
      <Toast message={message} error={error} />
    </section>
  )
}


import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import api from '../api'
import {
  Loader2,
  Trash2,
  ArrowLeft,
  Mail,
  Phone,
  Calendar,
  MessageSquare,
  Check,
  CheckCheck,
} from 'lucide-react'

const LuxuryContactMessages = () => {
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(null)
  const [readMessages, setReadMessages] = useState(new Set())

  const STORAGE_KEY = 'luxury_messages_read_status'

  const fetchMessages = async () => {
    try {
      const res = await api.get('/contact/messages')
      setMessages(res.data)

      const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)
      if (saved) {
        setReadMessages(new Set(JSON.parse(saved)))
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Failed to load messages:', err)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchMessages()
  }, [])

  const deleteMessage = async (id) => {
    await api.delete(`/contact/messages/${id}`)
    setMessages((prev) => prev.filter((m) => m.id !== id))
    if (selected?.id === id) setSelected(null)
  }

  const handleSelectMessage = async (message) => {
    setSelected(message)
    const newReadSet = new Set([...readMessages, message.id])
    setReadMessages(newReadSet)

    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify([...newReadSet]))

    try {
      await api.patch(`/contact/messages/${message.id}`, { read: true })
    } catch (err) {
      console.log('Message marked as read locally')
    }
  }

  const unreadCount = messages.filter((m) => !readMessages.has(m.id)).length

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 via-white to-slate-50">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-4">
          <Loader2 size={40} className="animate-spin text-emerald-500" />
          <p className="text-sm text-slate-600 font-semibold">Loading your messages</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 via-white to-slate-50 font-sans">
      <div
        className={`
          ${selected ? 'hidden' : 'flex'} 
          md:flex md:w-96 md:border-r md:border-slate-200/60 md:bg-white/40 md:backdrop-blur-xl md:flex-col
          w-full absolute md:relative h-full md:h-auto z-40 md:z-auto
          bg-white
        `}
      >
        <div className="px-4 md:px-6 py-6 md:py-8 border-b border-slate-200/60">
          <div className="flex items-baseline gap-3">
            <h1 className="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 font-serif tracking-tight">
              Messages
            </h1>
            {unreadCount > 0 && (
              <span className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full bg-rose-600 text-white text-xs font-black shadow-md shadow-rose-600/20">
                {unreadCount}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
          <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 mt-2">{messages.length} total</p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto scroll-smooth">
          {messages.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-full px-6">
              <div className="text-center">
                <MessageSquare size={40} className="mx-auto text-slate-300 mb-3" />
                <p className="text-slate-600 text-sm font-bold">No messages yet</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-1 p-4">
              {messages.map((message) => {
                const isSelected = selected?.id === message.id
                const isRead = readMessages.has(message.id)

                return (
                  <button
                    key={message.id}
                    onClick={() => handleSelectMessage(message)}
                    className={`w-full text-left px-4 py-3 rounded-2xl transition-all duration-200 group relative overflow-hidden ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-emerald-50 shadow-sm'
                        : 'hover:bg-slate-100/60 active:bg-slate-100'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {!isRead && (
                      <div className="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-1 bg-rose-500 transition-opacity duration-300" />
                    )}

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <div
                        className={`h-12 w-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-semibold text-white transition-all duration-200 flex-shrink-0 ${
                          isSelected
                            ? 'bg-emerald-500 shadow-md'
                            : 'bg-emerald-400/80 group-hover:bg-emerald-500'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {message.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                        <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
                          <p
                            className={`text-sm font-black truncate ${
                              isRead ? 'text-slate-700' : 'text-slate-900 font-black'
                            }`}
                          >
                            {message.name}
                          </p>
                          {!isRead && (
                            <span className="h-2.5 w-2.5 rounded-full bg-rose-500 flex-shrink-0" />
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-emerald-600 mt-0.5">
                          {message.inquiry_type}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 truncate mt-1">
                          {message.subject}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </button>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col bg-white/30 md:bg-white/30 backdrop-blur-sm w-full">
        {selected ? (
          <>
            <div className="border-b border-slate-200/40 px-4 md:px-8 py-4 md:py-6 flex items-center justify-between bg-white/20 md:bg-white/20 backdrop-blur-sm">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3 md:gap-4 min-w-0">
                <button
                  onClick={() => setSelected(null)}
                  className="md:hidden flex-shrink-0 p-2 hover:bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 rounded-lg transition-colors"
                >
                  <ArrowLeft size={20} />
                </button>

                <div className="h-12 md:h-14 w-12 md:w-14 rounded-full bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-400 to-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center text-white font-black shadow-md text-lg flex-shrink-0">
                  {selected.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                  <h2 className="text-lg md:text-2xl font-black text-slate-900 font-serif truncate">
                    {selected.subject}
                  </h2>
                  <p className="text-xs md:text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-2">
                    {selected.name}
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-600 font-bold">
                      <CheckCheck size={14} />
                      Read
                    </span>
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <button
                onClick={() => deleteMessage(selected.id)}
                className="p-2 md:p-2.5 hover:bg-red-50 text-red-600 rounded-xl transition-colors duration-200 active:scale-95 flex-shrink-0"
                title="Delete message"
              >
                <Trash2 size={20} />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-4 md:px-8 py-6 md:py-8">
              <div className="max-w-3xl space-y-6 md:space-y-8">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 md:gap-6">
                  <div className="bg-white/60 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-3 md:p-5 border border-slate-200/40">
                    <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-600 mb-2">
                      Email
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-slate-900 font-bold text-xs md:text-base break-all">
                      {selected.email}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  {selected.phone && (
                    <div className="bg-white/60 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-3 md:p-5 border border-slate-200/40">
                      <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-600 mb-2">
                        Phone
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-slate-900 font-bold text-xs md:text-base">
                        {selected.phone}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {selected.organization && (
                    <div className="bg-white/60 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-3 md:p-5 border border-slate-200/40">
                      <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-600 mb-2">
                        Organization
                      </p>
                      <p className="text-slate-900 font-bold text-xs md:text-base">
                        {selected.organization}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="bg-white/60 backdrop-blur-sm rounded-xl md:rounded-2xl p-3 md:p-5 border border-slate-200/40">
                    <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-600 mb-2">
                      Received
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-slate-900 font-bold text-xs md:text-base">
                      {new Date(selected.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-US', {
                        month: 'short',
                        day: 'numeric',
                        year: 'numeric',
                      })}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs font-black uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-600 mb-3">
                    Inquiry Type
                  </p>
                  <div className="inline-block">
