import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import {
  AlertCircle,
  ArrowDown,
  ArrowUp,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronDown,
  Eye,
  Loader2,
  Plus,
  Save,
  Sparkles,
  Trash2,
  Upload,
  Zap,
} from 'lucide-react'
import api from '../api'

// --------------- Helpers (identical to ImpactEditor) ---------------
const formInput =
  'w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/70 backdrop-blur px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 outline-none transition-all focus:border-emerald-400 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-emerald-500/10 focus:bg-white shadow-sm hover:border-emerald-300 hover:shadow-md'

const labelClass = 'mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600'

const Field = ({ label, children }) => (
  <label className="block">
    <span className={labelClass}>{label}</span>
    {children}
  </label>
)

const IconButton = ({ label, onClick, children, disabled, danger = false }) => (
  <button
    type="button"
    aria-label={label}
    disabled={disabled}
    onClick={onClick}
    className={`inline-flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-lg transition-all duration-200 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-40 ${
      danger
        ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100'
        : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 hover:bg-slate-200'
    }`}
  >
    {children}
  </button>
)

const Toast = ({ message, error }) => {
  if (!message && !error) return null
  return (
    <div
      className={`fixed bottom-6 right-6 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-medium shadow-lg ${
        error ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700' : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700'
      }`}
    >
      {error ? <AlertCircle size={18} /> : <CheckCircle2 size={18} />}
      <span>{message || error}</span>
    </div>
  )
}

const readLocalImage = (file, onLoad, onError) => {
  if (!file) return
  const reader = new FileReader()
  reader.onload = () => onLoad(String(reader.result || ''))
  reader.onerror = onError
  reader.readAsDataURL(file)
}

/* --------------- Reusable sub‑components (identical to ImpactEditor) --------------- */
function EditorShell({ title, saving, onSave, message, error, children }) {
  return (
    <section className="min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 to-slate-100/60">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-6 py-10">
        <div className="mb-10 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-start sm:items-center justify-between gap-6">
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-600 flex items-center gap-2">
              <Zap size={14} /> {title} Editor
            </p>
            <h1 className="mt-3 text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">{title}</h1>
          </div>
          <button
            onClick={onSave}
            disabled={saving}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-emerald-600 px-6 py-3 font-semibold text-white transition-all hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-emerald-500/30 active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {saving ? <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin" /> : <Save size={18} />}
            <span>{saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save All Changes'}</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_320px]">
          <div className="space-y-8">{children}</div>

          <aside className="space-y-6">
            <div className="sticky top-8 space-y-6">
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-md p-6 shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg transition-all">
                <h3 className="mb-4 text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-[0.2em] text-slate-400 flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Eye size={14} className="text-emerald-500" /> Live Preview
                </h3>
                <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-4 py-6 text-slate-900 min-h-[160px]">
                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-slate-500">
                    JOURNEY
                  </p>
                  <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
                    All changes are stored in the database.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="rounded-2xl border border-emerald-100 bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-50 to-emerald-100 p-6">
                <h3 className="mb-3 flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold text-emerald-900">
                  <Sparkles size={16} className="text-emerald-600" /> Editing Guide
                </h3>
                <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-emerald-800">
                  Click <strong>Save All Changes</strong> to persist your edits.
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
      <Toast message={message} error={error} />
    </section>
  )
}

function Card({ title, description, onAdd, children }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:shadow-lg hover:border-emerald-300">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-slate-200 pb-6">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">{title}</h2>
          {description && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">{description}</p>}
        </div>
        {onAdd && (
          <button
            onClick={onAdd}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-emerald-600 px-4 py-2 font-semibold text-white transition-all hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-emerald-500/30 active:scale-95"
          >
            <Plus size={18} /> Add
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}

function ExpandableItem({
  idx,
  expanded,
  setExpanded,
  headerLabel,
  headerValue,
  children,
  onUp,
  onDown,
  onDelete,
  upDisabled,
  downDisabled,
  isFocused,
  refProp,
}) {
  const isOpen = expanded.includes(String(idx))
  return (
    <div
      ref={isFocused ? refProp : null}
      className={`rounded-2xl border bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm shadow-sm transition-all duration-300 hover:shadow-lg hover:border-emerald-300 hover:scale-[1.01] cursor-pointer ${
        isFocused
          ? 'border-emerald-400 ring-2 ring-emerald-400/30 shadow-xl shadow-emerald-100/50'
          : 'border-slate-200'
      }`}
    >
      <button
        onClick={() =>
          setExpanded((prev) =>
            prev.includes(String(idx))
              ? prev.filter((i) => i !== String(idx))
              : [...prev, String(idx)],
          )
        }
        className="flex w-full items-center justify-between p-5 text-left"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
          <span className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-br from-emerald-400 to-emerald-600 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-md">
            {String(idx + 1).padStart(2, '0')}
          </span>
          <div>
            <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-emerald-600">
              {headerLabel}
            </p>
            <h3 className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900 line-clamp-1">{headerValue}</h3>
          </div>
        </div>
        <ChevronDown
          size={18}
          className={`text-slate-400 transition-transform duration-300 ${isOpen ? 'rotate-180' : ''}`}
        />
      </button>

      <div
        className={`overflow-hidden transition-all duration-500 ease-in-out ${isOpen ? 'max-h-[900px] opacity-100' : 'max-h-0 opacity-0'}`}
      >
        <div className="border-t border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50 p-5 space-y-4">
          {children}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <IconButton label="Move up" disabled={upDisabled} onClick={onUp}>
                <ArrowUp size={16} />
              </IconButton>
              <IconButton label="Move down" disabled={downDisabled} onClick={onDown}>
                <ArrowDown size={16} />
              </IconButton>
            </div>
            <IconButton label="Delete" danger onClick={onDelete}>
              <Trash2 size={16} />
            </IconButton>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

function EmptyState({ message }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-300 bg-slate-50 px-6 py-12 text-center hover:border-emerald-300 hover:bg-emerald-50 transition-all">
      <p className="text-slate-600">{message}</p>
    </div>
  )
}

/* --------------- Main Component --------------- */
export default function JourneyEditor() {
  const [content, setContent] = useState(null)
  const [draft, setDraft] = useState(null)
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true)
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [deletedIds, setDeletedIds] = useState([])

  // Expand/collapse states for each list
  const [expandedChapters, setExpandedChapters] = useState([])
  const [expandedPrinciples, setExpandedPrinciples] = useState([])
  const [expandedQuotes, setExpandedQuotes] = useState([])
  const [expandedEvents, setExpandedEvents] = useState([])

  // Focus for new items
  const newRef = useRef(null)
  const [focusId, setFocusId] = useState(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (focusId && newRef.current) {
      newRef.current.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' })
      const timer = setTimeout(() => newRef.current?.querySelector('input, textarea')?.focus(), 350)
      return () => clearTimeout(timer)
    }
    const reset = setTimeout(() => setFocusId(null), 2500)
    return () => clearTimeout(reset)
  }, [focusId])

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true
    const load = async () => {
      setIsLoading(true)
      try {
        const response = await api.get('/cms/site-content')
        if (!active) return
        setContent(response.data)
        const timeline = (response.data.timelineEvents || []).map((event) => ({
          ...event,
          year: event.year || '',
          title: event.title || '',
        }))
        const about = response.data.sections?.about_story || {}
        const philosophy = response.data.sections?.leadership_philosophy || {}
        const principles = response.data.sections?.about_values || {}
        const vision = response.data.sections?.about_vision || {}
        setDraft({
          events: timeline,
          about: {
            eyebrow: about.eyebrow || '',
            title: about.title || '',
            description: about.description || '',
            image_url: about.image_url || '',
            image_alt: about.image_alt || '',
            blocks: about.blocks || [],
          },
          principles: {
            eyebrow: principles.eyebrow || '',
            title: principles.title || '',
            description: principles.description || '',
            blocks: principles.blocks || [],
          },
          vision: {
            eyebrow: vision.eyebrow || '',
            title: vision.title || '',
            description: vision.description || '',
            blocks: vision.blocks || [],
          },
          philosophy: {
            eyebrow: philosophy.eyebrow || '',
            title: philosophy.title || '',
            description: philosophy.description || '',
            subtitle: philosophy.subtitle || '',
            blocks: philosophy.blocks || [],
          },
        })
      } catch (e) {
        if (!active) return
        setError('Unable to load Journey content.')
      } finally {
        if (active) setIsLoading(false)
      }
    }
    load()
    return () => {
      active = false
    }
  }, [])

  // --------------- Helper functions for each section ---------------
  // About Story
  const updateAbout = (patch) =>
    setDraft((current) => ({ ...current, about: { ...current.about, ...patch } }))
  const addChapter = () => {
    setDraft((current) => ({
      ...current,
      about: {
        ...current.about,
        blocks: [
          ...(current.about.blocks || []),
          {
            id: `new-${Date.now()}`,
            block_type: 'chapter',
            title: 'New Chapter',
            body: '',
            sort_order: (current.about.blocks || []).length + 1,
          },
        ],
      },
    }))
    setExpandedChapters((prev) => [...prev, String((draft?.about?.blocks || []).length)])
    setFocusId(`new-${Date.now()}`) // will be updated by the actual id generation; we can simplify
  }
  const updateChapter = (index, patch) =>
    setDraft((current) => ({
      ...current,
      about: {
        ...current.about,
        blocks: current.about.blocks.map((b, i) => (i === index ? { ...b, ...patch } : b)),
      },
    }))
  const removeChapter = (index) =>
    setDraft((current) => ({
      ...current,
      about: { ...current.about, blocks: current.about.blocks.filter((_, i) => i !== index) },
    }))
  const reorderChapter = (index, direction) =>
    setDraft((current) => {
      const blocks = [...current.about.blocks]
      const target = index + direction
      if (target < 0 || target >= blocks.length) return current
      const item = blocks[index]
      blocks[index] = blocks[target]
      blocks[target] = item
      return { ...current, about: { ...current.about, blocks } }
    })

  // Principles
  const updatePrinciples = (patch) =>
    setDraft((current) => ({ ...current, principles: { ...current.principles, ...patch } }))
  const addPrinciple = () => {
    setDraft((current) => ({
      ...current,
      principles: {
        ...current.principles,
        blocks: [
          ...(current.principles.blocks || []),
          {
            id: `new-${Date.now()}`,
            block_type: 'value',
            title: 'New Principle',
            body: '',
            sort_order: (current.principles.blocks || []).length + 1,
          },
        ],
      },
    }))
    setExpandedPrinciples((prev) => [...prev, String((draft?.principles?.blocks || []).length)])
    setFocusId(`new-${Date.now()}`)
  }
  const updatePrinciple = (index, patch) =>
    setDraft((current) => ({
      ...current,
      principles: {
        ...current.principles,
        blocks: current.principles.blocks.map((b, i) => (i === index ? { ...b, ...patch } : b)),
      },
    }))
  const removePrinciple = (index) =>
    setDraft((current) => ({
      ...current,
      principles: {
        ...current.principles,
        blocks: current.principles.blocks.filter((_, i) => i !== index),
      },
    }))
  const reorderPrinciple = (index, direction) =>
    setDraft((current) => {
      const blocks = [...current.principles.blocks]
      const target = index + direction
      if (target < 0 || target >= blocks.length) return current
      const item = blocks[index]
      blocks[index] = blocks[target]
      blocks[target] = item
      return { ...current, principles: { ...current.principles, blocks } }
    })

  // Leadership Philosophy
  const updatePhilosophy = (patch) =>
    setDraft((current) => ({ ...current, philosophy: { ...current.philosophy, ...patch } }))
  const addQuote = () => {
    setDraft((current) => ({
      ...current,
      philosophy: {
        ...current.philosophy,
        blocks: [
          ...(current.philosophy.blocks || []),
          {
            id: `new-${Date.now()}`,
            block_type: 'quote',
            body: '',
            title: '',
            sort_order: (current.philosophy.blocks || []).length + 1,
          },
        ],
      },
    }))
    setExpandedQuotes((prev) => [...prev, String((draft?.philosophy?.blocks || []).length)])
    setFocusId(`new-${Date.now()}`)
  }
  const updateQuote = (index, patch) =>
    setDraft((current) => ({
      ...current,
      philosophy: {
        ...current.philosophy,
        blocks: current.philosophy.blocks.map((b, i) => (i === index ? { ...b, ...patch } : b)),
      },
    }))
  const removeQuote = (index) =>
    setDraft((current) => ({
      ...current,
      philosophy: {
        ...current.philosophy,
        blocks: current.philosophy.blocks.filter((_, i) => i !== index),
      },
    }))
  const reorderQuote = (index, direction) =>
    setDraft((current) => {
      const blocks = [...current.philosophy.blocks]
      const target = index + direction
      if (target < 0 || target >= blocks.length) return current
      const item = blocks[index]
      blocks[index] = blocks[target]
      blocks[target] = item
      return { ...current, philosophy: { ...current.philosophy, blocks } }
    })

  // Vision
  const updateVision = (patch) =>
    setDraft((current) => ({ ...current, vision: { ...current.vision, ...patch } }))

  // Timeline Events
  const addEvent = () => {
    setDraft((current) => ({
      ...current,
      events: [
        ...(current.events || []),
        {
          id: `new-${Date.now()}`,
          year: '',
          title: '',
          sort_order: (current.events || []).length + 1,
        },
      ],
    }))
    setExpandedEvents((prev) => [...prev, String((draft?.events || []).length)])
    setFocusId(`new-${Date.now()}`)
  }
  const updateEvent = (index, patch) =>
    setDraft((current) => ({
      ...current,
      events: current.events.map((item, i) => (i === index ? { ...item, ...patch } : item)),
    }))
  const removeEvent = (index) => {
    setDraft((current) => {
      const removed = current.events[index]
      if (typeof removed?.id === 'number') setDeletedIds((ids) => [...ids, removed.id])
      return { ...current, events: current.events.filter((_, i) => i !== index) }
    })
  }
  const reorderEvent = (index, direction) =>
    setDraft((current) => {
      const next = [...current.events]
      const target = index + direction
      if (target < 0 || target >= next.length) return current
      const item = next[index]
      next[index] = next[target]
      next[target] = item
      return { ...current, events: next }
    })
  const updateEventImage = (index, file) =>
    readLocalImage(
      file,
      (url) => updateEvent(index, { image_url: url }),
      () => setError('Could not read image.'),
    )
  const updateAboutImage = (file) =>
    readLocalImage(
      file,
      (url) => updateAbout({ image_url: url }),
      () => setError('Could not read image.'),
    )

  // --------------- Save ---------------
  const save = async () => {
    if (!draft) return
    setMessage('')
    setError('')
    setIsSaving(true)
    try {
      for (const id of deletedIds) {
        await api.delete(`/cms/timeline-events/${id}`)
      }
      for (const [index, event] of (draft.events || []).entries()) {
        const payload = {
          slug: event.slug || null,
          year: event.year || null,
          title: event.title || '',
          sort_order: index + 1,
        }
        if (typeof event.id === 'number') await api.put(`/cms/timeline-events/${event.id}`, payload)
        else await api.post('/cms/timeline-events', payload)
      }
      if (draft.about) {
        await api.put('/cms/sections/about_story', {
          section: {
            eyebrow: draft.about.eyebrow,
            title: draft.about.title,
            description: draft.about.description,
            image_url: draft.about.image_url,
            image_alt: draft.about.image_alt,
          },
          blocks: draft.about.blocks || [],
        })
      }
      if (draft.vision) {
        await api.put('/cms/sections/about_vision', {
          section: {
            eyebrow: draft.vision.eyebrow,
            title: draft.vision.title,
            description: draft.vision.description,
          },
          blocks: draft.vision.blocks || [],
        })
      }
      if (draft.philosophy) {
        await api.put('/cms/sections/leadership_philosophy', {
          section: {
            eyebrow: draft.philosophy.eyebrow,
            title: draft.philosophy.title,
            description: draft.philosophy.description,
            subtitle: draft.philosophy.subtitle,
          },
          blocks: draft.philosophy.blocks || [],
        })
      }
      if (draft.principles) {
        await api.put('/cms/sections/about_values', {
          section: {
            eyebrow: draft.principles.eyebrow,
            title: draft.principles.title,
            description: draft.principles.description,
          },
          blocks: draft.principles.blocks || [],
        })
      }

      setMessage('✨ Journey saved successfully!')
      setDeletedIds([])
      setTimeout(() => setMessage(''), 3000)

      const response = await api.get('/cms/site-content')
      setContent(response.data)
      const timeline = (response.data.timelineEvents || []).map((event) => ({
        ...event,
        year: event.year || '',
        title: event.title || '',
      }))
      const about = response.data.sections?.about_story || {}
      const philosophy = response.data.sections?.leadership_philosophy || {}
      const principles = response.data.sections?.about_values || {}
      const vision = response.data.sections?.about_vision || {}
      setDraft({
        events: timeline,
        about: {
          eyebrow: about.eyebrow || '',
          title: about.title || '',
          description: about.description || '',
          image_url: about.image_url || '',
          image_alt: about.image_alt || '',
          blocks: about.blocks || [],
        },
        principles: {
          eyebrow: principles.eyebrow || '',
          title: principles.title || '',
          description: principles.description || '',
          blocks: principles.blocks || [],
        },
        vision: {
          eyebrow: vision.eyebrow || '',
          title: vision.title || '',
          description: vision.description || '',
          blocks: vision.blocks || [],
        },
        philosophy: {
          eyebrow: philosophy.eyebrow || '',
          title: philosophy.title || '',
          description: philosophy.description || '',
          subtitle: philosophy.subtitle || '',
          blocks: philosophy.blocks || [],
        },
      })
    } catch {
      setError('Could not save Journey. Please try again.')
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false)
    }
  }

  if (isLoading || !draft) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center">
        <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-emerald-500" size={32} />
        <p className="ml-3 text-slate-600">Loading Journey...</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <EditorShell title="Journey" saving={isSaving} onSave={save} message={message} error={error}>
      {/* About Story Card */}
      <Card
        title="About Story"
        description="Header and chapter blocks."
        onAdd={() => {
          addChapter()
        }}
      >
        <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2 mb-6">
          <Field label="Eyebrow">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={draft.about.eyebrow}
              onChange={(e) => updateAbout({ eyebrow: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Title (HTML)">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={draft.about.title}
              onChange={(e) => updateAbout({ title: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Image URL">
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <input
                className={formInput}
                value={draft.about.image_url || ''}
                onChange={(e) => updateAbout({ image_url: e.target.value })}
              />
              <label className="inline-flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 transition-all hover:bg-slate-200">
                <Upload size={18} />
                <input
                  type="file"
                  accept="image/*"
                  className="sr-only"
                  onChange={(e) => updateAboutImage(e.target.files?.[0])}
                />
              </label>
            </div>
          </Field>
          <Field label="Image Alt">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={draft.about.image_alt || ''}
              onChange={(e) => updateAbout({ image_alt: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <Field label="Description">
              <textarea
                className={formInput}
                rows={4}
                value={draft.about.description}
                onChange={(e) => updateAbout({ description: e.target.value })}
              />
            </Field>
          </div>
        </div>

        {draft.about.blocks.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState message="No chapters yet. Add one to get started." />
        ) : (
          draft.about.blocks.map((block, idx) => (
            <ExpandableItem
              key={block.id || idx}
              idx={idx}
              expanded={expandedChapters}
              setExpanded={setExpandedChapters}
              headerLabel={`Chapter ${idx + 1}`}
              headerValue={block.title || 'Untitled'}
              isFocused={focusId === block.id}
              refProp={newRef}
              onUp={() => reorderChapter(idx, -1)}
              onDown={() => reorderChapter(idx, 1)}
              onDelete={() => removeChapter(idx)}
              upDisabled={idx === 0}
              downDisabled={idx === draft.about.blocks.length - 1}
            >
              <Field label="Title">
                <input
                  className={formInput}
                  value={block.title}
                  onChange={(e) => updateChapter(idx, { title: e.target.value })}
                />
              </Field>
              <Field label="Body">
                <textarea
                  className={formInput}
                  rows={5}
                  value={block.body}
                  onChange={(e) => updateChapter(idx, { body: e.target.value })}
                />
              </Field>
              <Field label="Order">
                <input
                  className={formInput}
                  type="number"
                  value={block.sort_order || idx + 1}
                  onChange={(e) => updateChapter(idx, { sort_order: Number(e.target.value) })}
                />
              </Field>
            </ExpandableItem>
          ))
        )}
      </Card>

      {/* Principles Card */}
      <Card
        title="Principles (Values)"
        description="Core principles and values."
        onAdd={() => addPrinciple()}
      >
        <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2 mb-6">
          <Field label="Eyebrow">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={draft.principles.eyebrow}
              onChange={(e) => updatePrinciples({ eyebrow: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Title (HTML)">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={draft.principles.title}
              onChange={(e) => updatePrinciples({ title: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <Field label="Description">
              <textarea
                className={formInput}
                rows={4}
                value={draft.principles.description}
                onChange={(e) => updatePrinciples({ description: e.target.value })}
              />
            </Field>
          </div>
        </div>

        {draft.principles.blocks.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState message="No principles yet." />
        ) : (
          draft.principles.blocks.map((block, idx) => (
            <ExpandableItem
              key={block.id || idx}
              idx={idx}
              expanded={expandedPrinciples}
              setExpanded={setExpandedPrinciples}
              headerLabel="Principle"
              headerValue={block.title || 'Untitled'}
              isFocused={focusId === block.id}
              refProp={newRef}
              onUp={() => reorderPrinciple(idx, -1)}
              onDown={() => reorderPrinciple(idx, 1)}
              onDelete={() => removePrinciple(idx)}
              upDisabled={idx === 0}
              downDisabled={idx === draft.principles.blocks.length - 1}
            >
              <Field label="Title">
                <input
                  className={formInput}
                  value={block.title}
                  onChange={(e) => updatePrinciple(idx, { title: e.target.value })}
                />
              </Field>
              <Field label="Description">
                <textarea
                  className={formInput}
                  rows={4}
                  value={block.body}
                  onChange={(e) => updatePrinciple(idx, { body: e.target.value })}
                />
              </Field>
              <Field label="Order">
                <input
                  className={formInput}
                  type="number"
                  value={block.sort_order || idx + 1}
                  onChange={(e) => updatePrinciple(idx, { sort_order: Number(e.target.value) })}
                />
              </Field>
            </ExpandableItem>
          ))
        )}
      </Card>

      {/* Leadership Philosophy Card */}
      <Card
        title="Leadership Philosophy"
        description="Quotes and philosophy section."
        onAdd={() => addQuote()}
      >
        <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2 mb-6">
          <Field label="Eyebrow">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={draft.philosophy.eyebrow}
              onChange={(e) => updatePhilosophy({ eyebrow: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Title">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={draft.philosophy.title}
              onChange={(e) => updatePhilosophy({ title: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <Field label="Primary Quote">
              <textarea
                className={formInput}
                rows={4}
                value={draft.philosophy.description}
                onChange={(e) => updatePhilosophy({ description: e.target.value })}
              />
            </Field>
          </div>
          <Field label="Attribution">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={draft.philosophy.subtitle}
              onChange={(e) => updatePhilosophy({ subtitle: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
        </div>

        {draft.philosophy.blocks.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState message="No quotes yet." />
        ) : (
          draft.philosophy.blocks.map((block, idx) => (
            <ExpandableItem
              key={block.id || idx}
              idx={idx}
              expanded={expandedQuotes}
              setExpanded={setExpandedQuotes}
              headerLabel="Quote"
              headerValue={block.body ? block.body.slice(0, 50) + '...' : 'Untitled'}
              isFocused={focusId === block.id}
              refProp={newRef}
              onUp={() => reorderQuote(idx, -1)}
              onDown={() => reorderQuote(idx, 1)}
              onDelete={() => removeQuote(idx)}
              upDisabled={idx === 0}
              downDisabled={idx === draft.philosophy.blocks.length - 1}
            >
              <Field label="Quote Text">
                <textarea
                  className={formInput}
                  rows={4}
                  value={block.body}
                  onChange={(e) => updateQuote(idx, { body: e.target.value })}
                />
              </Field>
              <Field label="Attribution">
                <input
                  className={formInput}
                  value={block.title}
                  onChange={(e) => updateQuote(idx, { title: e.target.value })}
                />
              </Field>
            </ExpandableItem>
          ))
        )}
      </Card>

      {/* Vision Card */}
      <Card title="Vision">
        <div className="grid gap-6 md:grid-cols-2">
          <Field label="Eyebrow">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={draft.vision.eyebrow}
              onChange={(e) => updateVision({ eyebrow: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <Field label="Title">
            <input
              className={formInput}
              value={draft.vision.title}
              onChange={(e) => updateVision({ title: e.target.value })}
            />
          </Field>
          <div className="md:col-span-2">
            <Field label="Quote / Description">
              <textarea
                className={formInput}
                rows={4}
                value={draft.vision.description}
                onChange={(e) => updateVision({ description: e.target.value })}
              />
            </Field>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Card>

      {/* Timeline Events Card */}
      <Card title="Timeline Events" description="Chronological events." onAdd={() => addEvent()}>
        {draft.events.length === 0 ? (
          <EmptyState message="No events yet." />
        ) : (
          draft.events.map((event, idx) => (
            <ExpandableItem
              key={event.id || idx}
              idx={idx}
              expanded={expandedEvents}
              setExpanded={setExpandedEvents}
              headerLabel={event.year || 'Year'}
              headerValue={event.title || 'Untitled Event'}
              isFocused={focusId === event.id}
              refProp={newRef}
              onUp={() => reorderEvent(idx, -1)}
              onDown={() => reorderEvent(idx, 1)}
              onDelete={() => removeEvent(idx)}
              upDisabled={idx === 0}
              downDisabled={idx === draft.events.length - 1}
            >
              <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                <Field label="Year">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={event.year}
                    onChange={(e) => updateEvent(idx, { year: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Title">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={event.title}
                    onChange={(e) => updateEvent(idx, { title: e.target.value })}
                  />
                </Field>
              </div>
              {/* Image upload for event */}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <Field label="Image">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={event.image_url || ''}
                    onChange={(e) => updateEvent(idx, { image_url: e.target.value })}
                    placeholder="Image URL"
                  />
                </Field>
                <label className="inline-flex h-11 w-11 shrink-0 cursor-pointer items-center justify-center rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 transition-all hover:bg-slate-200 mt-5">
                  <Upload size={18} />
                  <input
                    type="file"
                    accept="image/*"
                    className="sr-only"
                    onChange={(e) => updateEventImage(idx, e.target.files?.[0])}
                  />
                </label>
              </div>
            </ExpandableItem>
          ))
        )}
      </Card>
    </EditorShell>
  )
}


import { Lock, LogIn, Mail, Zap } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import api from '../api'

export default function Login() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const navigate = useNavigate()

  const handleSubmit = async (event) => {
    event.preventDefault()
    setError('')
    setIsSubmitting(true)

    try {
      const response = await api.post('/auth/login', { email, password })
      localStorage.setItem('token', response.data.token)
      navigate('/admin')
    } catch {
      setError('Invalid email or password.')
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <main className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center bg-gradient-to-br from-slate-50 to-slate-100/60 px-5 py-12">
      <section className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 backdrop-blur-sm p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
        {/* Header */}
        <div className="mb-8 border-b border-slate-200 pb-7">
          <div className="mb-4 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Zap size={16} className="text-emerald-600" />
            <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-emerald-600">
              Admin Access
            </p>
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold text-slate-900">Sign in to CMS</h1>
          <p className="mt-3 text-sm font-light leading-relaxed text-slate-600">
            Manage your portfolio content with the same editorial precision as the public site.
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Form */}
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-6">
          {/* Email field */}
          <label className="block">
            <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600">
              Email
            </span>
            <div className="relative">
              <Mail size={16} className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
              <input
                type="email"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/70 backdrop-blur pl-11 pr-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 outline-none transition-all focus:border-emerald-400 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-emerald-500/10 focus:bg-white shadow-sm hover:border-emerald-300 hover:shadow-md"
                placeholder="admin@example.com"
                required
              />
            </div>
          </label>

          {/* Password field */}
          <label className="block">
            <span className="mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600">
              Password
            </span>
            <div className="relative">
              <Lock size={16} className="absolute left-4 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
              <input
                type="password"
                value={password}
                onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/70 backdrop-blur pl-11 pr-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 outline-none transition-all focus:border-emerald-400 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-emerald-500/10 focus:bg-white shadow-sm hover:border-emerald-300 hover:shadow-md"
                placeholder="Enter password"
                required
              />
            </div>
          </label>

          {/* Error */}
          {error && (
            <div className="rounded-xl border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-red-700">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Submit button */}
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-emerald-600 px-6 py-3 font-semibold text-white transition-all hover:shadow-xl hover:shadow-emerald-500/30 active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
          >
            <LogIn size={18} />
            <span>{isSubmitting ? 'Signing in…' : 'Login'}</span>
          </button>
        </form>
      </section>
    </main>
  )
}


import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react'
import {
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  GraduationCap,
  Images,
  Loader2,
  Save,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import api from '../api'
import JourneyEditor from './JourneyEditor.jsx'
import {
  education as fallbackEducation,
  professionalMemberships as fallbackMemberships,
} from '@/data/education'
import { galleryItems as fallbackGallery } from '@/data/gallery'

const sections = [
  { slug: 'journey', title: 'Journey', icon: Sparkles },
  { slug: 'education', title: 'Education', icon: GraduationCap },
  { slug: 'gallery', title: 'Gallery', icon: Images },
]

const formInput =
  'w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/70 backdrop-blur px-4 py-3 text-sm text-slate-900 placeholder:text-slate-400 outline-none transition-all focus:border-teal-400 focus:ring-4 focus:ring-teal-500/10 focus:bg-white shadow-sm hover:border-teal-300 hover:shadow-md'
const labelClass = 'mb-2 block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-600'

function Field({ label, children, hint }) {
  return (
    <label className="block">
      <span className={labelClass}>{label}</span>
      {children}
      {hint && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">{hint}</p>}
    </label>
  )
}

function Toast({ message, error }) {
  if (!message && !error) return null
  return (
    <div
      className={`fixed bottom-6 right-6 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-5 py-3.5 text-sm font-medium shadow-lg transition-all duration-300 ${
        error ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-700' : 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700'
      }`}
    >
      {error ? <AlertCircle size={18} /> : <CheckCircle2 size={18} />}
      <span>{message || error}</span>
    </div>
  )
}

const normalizeEducation = (entry) => ({
  id: entry.id || `edu-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`,
  institution: entry.institution || '',
  degree: entry.degree || '',
  field: entry.field || '',
  year: entry.year || new Date().getFullYear(),
  description: entry.description || '',
  highlights: Array.isArray(entry.highlights) ? entry.highlights : [],
})

const normalizeGallery = (entry) => ({
  id: entry.id || `gallery-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)}`,
  src: entry.src || '',
  alt: entry.alt || '',
  caption: entry.caption || '',
  category: entry.category || 'community',
  date: entry.date || '',
  location: entry.location || '',
})

export default function ProfileEditor() {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const { sectionSlug } = useParams()
  const activeSection = useMemo(
    () => sections.find((section) => section.slug === sectionSlug) ?? sections[0],
    [sectionSlug],
  )

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sectionSlug) navigate('/admin/profile/journey', { replace: true })
  }, [navigate, sectionSlug])

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const [educationSection, setEducationSection] = useState({
    eyebrow: 'Credentials',
    title: 'Academic foundations',
    description: 'Academic training and professional credentials that shaped the journey.',
    membershipsEyebrow: 'Affiliations',
    membershipsTitle: 'Professional memberships',
    membershipsDescription:
      'Active roles across national and international professional bodies in healthcare leadership and community service.',
    education: fallbackEducation.map(normalizeEducation),
    memberships: [...fallbackMemberships],
  })

  const [gallerySection, setGallerySection] = useState({
    eyebrow: 'Visual Archive',
    title: 'A visual journey',
    description:
      'A curated archive of milestones, leadership moments, and community impact captured over the years.',
    items: fallbackGallery.map(normalizeGallery),
  })

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true
    const load = async () => {
      setLoading(true)
      setError('')
      try {
        const response = await api.get('/cms/site-content')
        if (!active) return
        const sectionsData = response.data?.sections || {}
        const aboutEducation = sectionsData.about_education || {}
        const aboutGallery = sectionsData.about_gallery || {}

        setEducationSection((current) => ({
          ...current,
          eyebrow: aboutEducation.eyebrow || current.eyebrow,
          title: aboutEducation.title || current.title,
          description: aboutEducation.description || current.description,
          membershipsEyebrow: String(
            aboutEducation.metadata?.membership_eyebrow || current.membershipsEyebrow,
          ),
          membershipsTitle: String(
            aboutEducation.metadata?.membership_title || current.membershipsTitle,
          ),
          membershipsDescription: String(
            aboutEducation.metadata?.membership_description || current.membershipsDescription,
          ),
          education:
            Array.isArray(aboutEducation.blocks) && aboutEducation.blocks.length > 0
              ? aboutEducation.blocks.map((block) => ({
                  id: String(block.id),
                  institution: block.title || '',
                  degree: String(block.metadata?.degree || ''),
                  field: String(block.metadata?.field || ''),
                  year: Number(block.metadata?.year || new Date().getFullYear()),
                  description: block.body || '',
                  highlights: Array.isArray(block.metadata?.highlights)
                    ? block.metadata.highlights
                    : [],
                }))
              : current.education,
          memberships: Array.isArray(aboutEducation.metadata?.memberships)
            ? aboutEducation.metadata.memberships
            : current.memberships,
        }))

        setGallerySection((current) => ({
          ...current,
          eyebrow: aboutGallery.eyebrow || current.eyebrow,
          title: aboutGallery.title || current.title,
          description: aboutGallery.description || current.description,
          items:
            Array.isArray(aboutGallery.blocks) && aboutGallery.blocks.length > 0
              ? aboutGallery.blocks.map((block) => ({
                  id: String(block.id),
                  src: block.image_url || '',
                  alt: block.image_alt || block.title || '',
                  caption: block.body || '',
                  category: String(block.metadata?.category || 'community'),
                  date: String(block.metadata?.date || ''),
                  location: String(block.metadata?.location || ''),
                }))
              : current.items,
        }))
      } catch {
        setError('Could not load profile content. Using defaults.')
      } finally {
        if (active) setLoading(false)
      }
    }
    load()
    return () => {
      active = false
    }
  }, [])

  const saveSection = async () => {
    setSaving(true)
    setMessage('')
    setError('')
    try {
      if (activeSection.slug === 'education') {
        await api.put('/cms/sections/about_education', {
          section: {
            eyebrow: educationSection.eyebrow,
            title: educationSection.title,
            description: educationSection.description,
            metadata: {
              membership_eyebrow: educationSection.membershipsEyebrow,
              membership_title: educationSection.membershipsTitle,
              membership_description: educationSection.membershipsDescription,
              memberships: educationSection.memberships,
            },
          },
          blocks: educationSection.education.map((item) => ({
            title: item.institution,
            body: item.description,
            metadata: {
              degree: item.degree,
              field: item.field,
              year: item.year,
              highlights: item.highlights,
            },
          })),
        })
      } else if (activeSection.slug === 'gallery') {
        await api.put('/cms/sections/about_gallery', {
          section: {
            eyebrow: gallerySection.eyebrow,
            title: gallerySection.title,
            description: gallerySection.description,
          },
          blocks: gallerySection.items.map((item) => ({
            title: item.alt || item.caption,
            body: item.caption,
            image_url: item.src,
            image_alt: item.alt,
            metadata: {
              category: item.category,
              date: item.date,
              location: item.location,
            },
          })),
        })
      }
      setMessage(`${activeSection.title} saved.`)
    } catch {
      setError('Could not save profile content.')
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-80 items-center justify-center border border-rule bg-paper-card">
        <Loader2 size={26} className="animate-spin text-teal" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  const journeySelected = activeSection.slug === 'journey'
  const educationSelected = activeSection.slug === 'education'
  const gallerySelected = activeSection.slug === 'gallery'

  return (
    <section className="mx-auto max-w-6xl">
      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 border-b border-slate-200 pb-7 md:flex-row md:items-end">
        <div>
          <p className="section-tag flex items-center gap-2">
            <Sparkles size={14} />
            Profile Editor
          </p>
          <h1 className="mt-3 font-serif text-4xl font-light text-ink sm:text-5xl">
            {activeSection.title}
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-3 max-w-2xl text-sm font-light leading-relaxed text-ink-mid">
            Edit one profile section at a time using the sidebar selection.
          </p>
        </div>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={saveSection}
          disabled={saving || journeySelected}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 self-start bg-teal px-5 py-2.5 font-sans text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-[0.18em] text-white transition-colors hover:bg-teal-mid disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-70"
        >
          {saving ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin" /> : <Save size={16} />}
          {journeySelected ? 'Use Journey Save' : saving ? 'Saving' : 'Save Section'}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-wrap gap-2 border-b border-rule pb-4">
        {sections.map((section) => {
          const Icon = section.icon
          const active = activeSection.slug === section.slug
          return (
            <button
              key={section.slug}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate(`/admin/profile/${section.slug}`)}
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 rounded-xl px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-[0.18em] transition-colors ${
                active ? 'bg-teal text-white' : 'border border-rule text-ink-muted hover:text-ink'
              }`}
            >
              <Icon size={14} />
              {section.title}
            </button>
          )
        })}
      </div>

      {journeySelected && (
        <div className="rounded-2xl border border-rule bg-paper-card p-6">
          <JourneyEditor />
        </div>
      )}

      {educationSelected && (
        <div className="grid gap-8 xl:grid-cols-[1fr_340px]">
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <section className="group rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 p-6 shadow-sm backdrop-blur-sm sm:p-8">
              <div className="mb-5 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">Education Hero Copy</h2>
              </div>
              <div className="grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
                <Field label="Eyebrow">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={educationSection.eyebrow}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setEducationSection((current) => ({ ...current, eyebrow: e.target.value }))
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Headline">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={educationSection.title}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setEducationSection((current) => ({ ...current, title: e.target.value }))
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                  <Field label="Description">
                    <textarea
                      className={formInput}
                      rows={4}
                      value={educationSection.description}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setEducationSection((current) => ({
                          ...current,
                          description: e.target.value,
                        }))
                      }
                    />
                  </Field>
                </div>
                <Field label="Membership Eyebrow">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={educationSection.membershipsEyebrow}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setEducationSection((current) => ({
                        ...current,
                        membershipsEyebrow: e.target.value,
                      }))
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Membership Title">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={educationSection.membershipsTitle}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setEducationSection((current) => ({
                        ...current,
                        membershipsTitle: e.target.value,
                      }))
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                  <Field label="Membership Description">
                    <textarea
                      className={formInput}
                      rows={3}
                      value={educationSection.membershipsDescription}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setEducationSection((current) => ({
                          ...current,
                          membershipsDescription: e.target.value,
                        }))
                      }
                    />
                  </Field>
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>

            <section className="group rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 p-6 shadow-sm backdrop-blur-sm sm:p-8">
              <div className="mb-5 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">Education Entries</h2>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {educationSection.education.map((item, index) => (
                  <div key={item.id} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 p-4">
                    <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                      <Field label="Institution">
                        <input
                          className={formInput}
                          value={item.institution}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setEducationSection((current) => ({
                              ...current,
                              education: current.education.map((row, i) =>
                                i === index ? { ...row, institution: e.target.value } : row,
                              ),
                            }))
                          }
                        />
                      </Field>
                      <Field label="Degree">
                        <input
                          className={formInput}
                          value={item.degree}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setEducationSection((current) => ({
                              ...current,
                              education: current.education.map((row, i) =>
                                i === index ? { ...row, degree: e.target.value } : row,
                              ),
                            }))
                          }
                        />
                      </Field>
                      <Field label="Field">
                        <input
                          className={formInput}
                          value={item.field}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setEducationSection((current) => ({
                              ...current,
                              education: current.education.map((row, i) =>
                                i === index ? { ...row, field: e.target.value } : row,
                              ),
                            }))
                          }
                        />
                      </Field>
                      <Field label="Year">
                        <input
                          className={formInput}
                          type="number"
                          value={item.year}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setEducationSection((current) => ({
                              ...current,
                              education: current.education.map((row, i) =>
                                i === index ? { ...row, year: Number(e.target.value) } : row,
                              ),
                            }))
                          }
                        />
                      </Field>
                      <div className="md:col-span-2">
                        <Field label="Description">
                          <textarea
                            className={formInput}
                            rows={3}
                            value={item.description}
                            onChange={(e) =>
                              setEducationSection((current) => ({
                                ...current,
                                education: current.education.map((row, i) =>
                                  i === index ? { ...row, description: e.target.value } : row,
                                ),
                              }))
                            }
                          />
                        </Field>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          </div>

          <aside className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 p-5 shadow-sm backdrop-blur-sm sm:p-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
              <GraduationCap size={18} className="text-teal" />
              <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl font-light text-ink">Education Preview</h3>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-ink-muted">
              {educationSection.eyebrow}
            </p>
            <p className="font-serif text-3xl font-light text-ink">{educationSection.title}</p>
            <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-ink-mid">{educationSection.description}</p>
          </aside>
        </div>
      )}

      {gallerySelected && (
        <div className="grid gap-8 xl:grid-cols-[1fr_340px]">
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <section className="group rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 p-6 shadow-sm backdrop-blur-sm sm:p-8">
              <div className="mb-5 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">Gallery Hero Copy</h2>
              </div>
              <div className="grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
                <Field label="Eyebrow">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={gallerySection.eyebrow}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setGallerySection((current) => ({ ...current, eyebrow: e.target.value }))
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
                <Field label="Headline">
                  <input
                    className={formInput}
                    value={gallerySection.title}
                    onChange={(e) =>
                      setGallerySection((current) => ({ ...current, title: e.target.value }))
                    }
                  />
                </Field>
                <div className="md:col-span-2">
                  <Field label="Description">
                    <textarea
                      className={formInput}
                      rows={4}
                      value={gallerySection.description}
                      onChange={(e) =>
                        setGallerySection((current) => ({
                          ...current,
                          description: e.target.value,
                        }))
                      }
                    />
                  </Field>
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>

            <section className="group rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 p-6 shadow-sm backdrop-blur-sm sm:p-8">
              <div className="mb-5 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
                <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900">Gallery Entries</h2>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {gallerySection.items.map((item, index) => (
                  <div key={item.id} className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 p-4">
                    <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
                      <Field label="Image URL">
                        <input
                          className={formInput}
                          value={item.src}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setGallerySection((current) => ({
                              ...current,
                              items: current.items.map((row, i) =>
                                i === index ? { ...row, src: e.target.value } : row,
                              ),
                            }))
                          }
                        />
                      </Field>
                      <Field label="Alt Text">
                        <input
                          className={formInput}
                          value={item.alt}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setGallerySection((current) => ({
                              ...current,
                              items: current.items.map((row, i) =>
                                i === index ? { ...row, alt: e.target.value } : row,
                              ),
                            }))
                          }
                        />
                      </Field>
                      <Field label="Category">
                        <select
                          className={formInput}
                          value={item.category}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setGallerySection((current) => ({
                              ...current,
                              items: current.items.map((row, i) =>
                                i === index ? { ...row, category: e.target.value } : row,
                              ),
                            }))
                          }
                        >
                          {[
                            'leadership',
                            'healthcare',
                            'education',
                            'awards',
                            'community',
                            'conferences',
                          ].map((category) => (
                            <option key={category} value={category}>
                              {category}
                            </option>
                          ))}
                        </select>
                      </Field>
                      <Field label="Date">
                        <input
                          className={formInput}
                          value={item.date}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setGallerySection((current) => ({
                              ...current,
                              items: current.items.map((row, i) =>
                                i === index ? { ...row, date: e.target.value } : row,
                              ),
                            }))
                          }
                        />
                      </Field>
                      <Field label="Location">
                        <input
                          className={formInput}
                          value={item.location}
                          onChange={(e) =>
                            setGallerySection((current) => ({
                              ...current,
                              items: current.items.map((row, i) =>
                                i === index ? { ...row, location: e.target.value } : row,
                              ),
                            }))
                          }
                        />
                      </Field>
                      <div className="md:col-span-2">
                        <Field label="Caption">
                          <textarea
                            className={formInput}
                            rows={3}
                            value={item.caption}
                            onChange={(e) =>
                              setGallerySection((current) => ({
                                ...current,
                                items: current.items.map((row, i) =>
                                  i === index ? { ...row, caption: e.target.value } : row,
                                ),
                              }))
                            }
                          />
                        </Field>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          </div>

          <aside className="space-y-4 rounded-2xl border border-slate-200 bg-white/80 p-5 shadow-sm backdrop-blur-sm sm:p-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-slate-200 pb-4">
              <Images size={18} className="text-teal" />
              <h3 className="font-serif text-2xl font-light text-ink">Gallery Preview</h3>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-[0.22em] text-ink-muted">
              {gallerySection.eyebrow}
            </p>
            <p className="font-serif text-3xl font-light text-ink">{gallerySection.title}</p>
            <p className="text-sm leading-relaxed text-ink-mid">{gallerySection.description}</p>
          </aside>
        </div>
      )}

      <Toast message={message} error={error} />
    </section>
  )
}

import {
  ArrowDown,
  ArrowUp,
  BriefcaseBusiness,
  GraduationCap,
  Handshake,
  HeartPulse,
  Hospital,
  Landmark,
  Loader2,
  Microscope,
  Plus,
  Save,
  Smartphone,
  Sparkles,
  Hammer,
  Trash2,
  Trophy,
  Upload,
  Wallet,
  AlertCircle,
  CheckCircle2,
  ChevronDown,
  Zap,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import api from '../api'

// ------------------------------------------------------------------
// CONSTANTS & HELPERS
// ------------------------------------------------------------------

  {
    slug: 'vision-agenda',
    title: 'The Visionary Agenda',
    description: 'Hero story opener with identity cards and bold positioning.',
    backendSlug: 'vision_agenda',
    hint: 'Fill in each headline field separately using plain text only — no HTML or code. The "Highlighted" word(s) will appear in a custom colour automatically.',
  },
  {
    slug: 'career-timeline',
    title: 'Career Timeline',
    description: 'Chronologically manage story beats and milestone events.',
    type: 'timeline',
    hint: 'Add, reorder, and update career timeline events.',
  },
  {
    slug: 'liyana-framework',
    title: 'The Liyana Healthcare Framework',
    description: 'Manage the framework grid, ordering, descriptions, and selected icons.',

  },
  {

    hint: 'Edit the heading, intro text, pillars, and stat cards. Each pillar has a title and an icon.',

  {

  },
  {
    slug: 'leadership-philosophy',
    title: 'Leadership Philosophy',
    description: 'Manage the full-width quote section and attribution.',

    hint: 'Write the quote and attribution as plain text. The styling is applied automatically on the website.',
  },
  {
    slug: 'testimonials',
    title: 'Testimonials',
    description: 'Edit the section heading and call‑to‑action.',
